Would you be ok if I use them for int and con in that case?</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 8:50 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">One random thought...<div><br></div><div>The entire party right now is CHA-focused at least a little bit... if you wanted to play an INT-focused sorcerer I'd be happy to make that work. That is, a sorcerer who's spellcasting stat is INT rather than CHA. An INT-based lunar dwarven sorcerer who got magic by studying the lunar cycles would be a good fit to the rest of the party and seems like it could be fun.</div><div><br></div><div>But it's just a random&nbsp;idea, you are welcome to keep up the CHA-trend in the party too. That will work fine :)&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div><br></div></div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Thu, Dec 29, 2022 at 2:07 PM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto"><br><div dir="ltr"><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 29, 2022, at 12:30 PM, Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿<div dir="auto">A couple of thoughts:</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">1. How would you feel about making the moon's blessing cantrip a spell attack instead of a save or suck? It's probably equivalent from a math perspective but it just feels better to be in control of whether it hits or not.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>Fine with me.&nbsp;</div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">2. For the spell lists we could look at the book for inspiration I think the bonus spells in particular we can pull heavily from there. They use a mix of spells including some not normally on the sorcerer's spell list if I recall correctly. May as well not reinvent the wheel.</div><div dir="auto"><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>I can look especially if you want to send me a screenshot of that section or something. But also there is a specific Taelgar phenomenon at work here — during the new moon the planar tides pull strongly at the elemental and fey planes, so elemental and fey magic is stronger. During the full moon the tides are pulling the divine/spiritual realms closer, so more divine flavored magic is stronger. This isn’t a phenomenon that affects all spellcasters but it would impact this particular subclass so I want to try to align the bonus spells to that theory.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">3. For lunar vision normally that would stack on top of dark vision but it's slightly different, so of i choose a race with dark vision does it become 90 or are they different? If it stacks i would just say "gain dark vision up to 30 feet and in addition moonlight counts as daylight for you" so the rest of it docks into the RAW more easily.</div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>Let’s discuss if it comes up. I won’t make it useless if you end up taking a dwarf though.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">4. For the 6th level feature I love the flavor. I wonder if there's a way to make it not totally useless in the opposite phase for a given spell? It would feel unfortunate if the one spell I needed just was out of phase for this. Like would it be balanced to divvy up the metamagic feats themselves instead of the spells colleges? That way I can reduce the cost of these metamagics on half the month and vice versa but the spells can be whatever.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>I don’t love the game flavor of dividing up metamagic as the point is to align the spells with the planar tides described above. But if the flip-flop nature seems annoying I can brainstorm other ideas. It will be a while before the 6th level feature comes into play.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">Race I'm debating a bit. Originally leaning towards human, but also considering Dwarf. Alternatively there are a couple of fun races that offer flight which is fun but I don't know if any of them work in this world. </div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>I’m open to anything but human or dwarf is certainly easier to fit into the world.&nbsp;</div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">Background would depend a bit on the race obviously but I'm thinking some kind of druidic / priesthood style order of the moon? Is there room for that sort of thing? Or would I be more of a solo champion?</div><div><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div>Again, open to anything but a solo champion so to speak fits into the plot a bit better.&nbsp;<div><br></div><div>Mike<br><div><br></div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Thu, Dec 29, 2022 at 10:54 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">Matt,<div><br></div><div>I haven't made up more than the initial bits, but here is what I'm thinking for lunar sorcerer. The version below is only made up through 6th level and leans heavily into different abilities for different phases of the moon. I am happy to set up a moon calendar so we know which phases of the moon are which (probably by just picking an Earth year and using that) but I can't quite decide if it will be too annoying to track.</div><div><br></div><div>What do you think of the below? I'm still tweaking a bit so might play with the power levels a bit and totally happy to get feedback. If you like it I'll make it in D&amp;D Beyond.</div><div><br></div><div><b>1st Level Power</b></div><div>You gain a new cantrip, <i>moon's blessing</i>, that allows you to call forth the power of the moon to attack your enemies, and additionally, you gain a set of bonus spells.</div><div><br></div><div><b><i>Moon's Blessing</i></b></div><div>You call forth the blessing of the moon to attack your enemies. You choose an enemy within 60', who must make a DEX save or take 1d8 damage. The spell damage increases by 1d8 at 5th, 11th, and 17th level.</div><div><br></div><div>During the full moon, and the week around it, the lunar tides pull strongly on the planes of the gods, and the damage is radiant, and you gain the ability to target a second creature within 5'.</div><div><br></div><div>During the new moon, and the week around it, the lunar tides pull strongly on the elemental planes, the damage is elemental, channeled from the elemental planes. You may choose which plane to channel when you cast the spell:&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>From the plane of fire, the spell does fire damage.</div><div>From the plane of water, the spell does cold damage.</div><div>From the plane of air, the spell does thunder or lightning damage, your choice</div><div>From the plane of earth, the spell does bludgeoning damage and deals double damage to object or structures</div><div><br></div><div>In between, when the moon is a crescent, the tides are balanced, and the spell does force damage, and has a range of 90' instead of 60'</div><div><br></div><div><b><i>Lunar Vision</i></b></div><div>You gain the ability to see in the moonlight as well as the daylight. You can see in dim light as well as full light, and in darkness, out to a range of 30'</div><div><b><br></b></div><div><b>Bonus Spells</b></div><div><b><br></b></div><div>You gain three bonus spells for each level, which count as sorcerer spells for you, and which do not count towards your known spells. These spells are divided by phase of the moon: one spell of each level is a full moon spell, one a new moon spell, and one a neutral spell. During the week around the new moon, and the week of the waning moon before that, you may not cast the full moon spell. During the week of the full moon, and the week of the waxing moon before that, you may not cast the new moon spell. So in effect, for half the month you have the new moon spell and half the full moon, plus you always have access to the neutral spell.</div><div><br></div><div>1st Level:</div><div>New Moon: Charm Person</div><div>Full Moon: Sanctuary</div><div>Neutral: TBD</div><div><b><br></b></div><div>3rd Level:</div><div>New Moon: TBD</div><div>etc</div><div><br></div><div><b>6th Level</b></div><div>At 6th level, you start to be able to see and manipulate the lunar planar tides more directly to help your magic.</div><div><br></div><div>When the moon is full, and for the week around it, when casting spells of the Abjuration, Necromancy, Evocation, or Divination schools you can reduce the cost of any metamagic by 1 min 0.</div><div><br></div><div>When the moon is new, and for the week around it, when casting spells of the Enchantment, Conjuration, Illusion, or Transmutation schools you can reduce the cost of any metamagic by 1 min 0.</div><div><br></div><div>You can use this feature a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus, and regain all uses after a long rest.</div><div><br></div><div>[Obviously this effect is useless half the month; either I will expand so that each version is 50% of the time, or split the spells into 3 groups with one group for the middle, or add some other ability for the middle point in time.. any thoughts?]</div><div><br></div><div>Haven't thought about 14th or higher but obviously a long way away :)</div><div><br></div><div>Any thoughts on race and background?&nbsp;</div></div><div dir="ltr"><div><br></div><div>Mike</div></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</div></blockquote></div></div></blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</div>
</div>
</div></blockquote></div></blockquote></div></div></div></div></div></div></blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div>
</div></blockquote></div></div></blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</div></blockquote></body></html>

## Sat, 31 Dec 2022 09:10:11 -0500 — Mike Sackton <msackton@gmail.com>

Subject: Re: Lunar Sorceror

(HTML source)

<html><head><meta http-equiv="content-type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"></head><body dir="auto">I was also thinking of tweaking moon’s pull so that instead of a sphere/damage it was something like:<div><br></div><div>Target a single creature or object. Duration 1 minute. Creature takes 2d8 dmg on failed save, then is slowed for the duration. Object cannot be lifted unless someone makes str save. Also you could use it to make things heavier, if say you wanted to trigger a pit trap with a small rock you could cast this to make the rock much heavier.&nbsp;<br><br><div dir="ltr">Sent from my iPhone</div><div dir="ltr"><br><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 31, 2022, at 8:37 AM, Matthew Rand &lt;rand.matt@gmail.com&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿<div>I like it!</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">Only thing is the new moon spell jives with my mental idea of the moon as the source of magic. Did you want to play more with elemental in the new moon one?</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:23 PM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">I added a Lunar Sorcery (Taelgar) subclass. It doesn't have everything, in particular it is still missing bonus spells or 6th/14/th/18th level features.<div><br></div><div>I changed the darkvision thing to Lunar Sight -- you can see in moonlight as daylight, plus you get advantage on perception checks at night.</div><div><br></div><div>For bonus spells, for 1st level I was thinking:</div><div><br></div><div>Full Moon: Sanctuary</div><div>New Moon: Dissonant Whispers</div><div>Half-Moon (always): Magnify Gravity</div><div><br></div><div>Thoughts?</div></div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 6:13 PM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">I'm thinking he wasn't directly involved in the war. Mostly would have been drafted into supply caravans and working as a quartermaster - things that would have used his skills but kept him out of the fighting. To me he basically lived most of his life as an unleveled civilian and only recently did he become anything approaching heroic which is how he's as old as he is and still only level 1.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">Also good notes on the languages. I also was looking at the homebrew race template and wasn't sure if it would be cool for him to have two runes he can inscribe (buy has to choose one each day) given his split history. I was thinking the Rune of Lore (more recently) and the Rune of Exploration from his previous life?</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 4:25 PM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">Level 1 to start but you’ll level up to 2nd almost immediately.&nbsp;<div><br></div><div>For languages I think Drankorian (the ancient Latin analogue) might be interesting as could giant (there are legends that the giants had interesting runes/scholarship) or Chardonian (the language of the current main scholar city)</div><div><br></div><div>Highlander would also definitely be something he would know— it’s the main language of the humans who live closest to the dwarves.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>Mike</div><div><br><div dir="ltr">Sent from my iPhone</div><div dir="ltr"><br><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 30, 2022, at 3:49 PM, Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿</div></blockquote></div></div><div dir="auto"><div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr">And lastly, let me know when you've created the subclass in D&amp;D beyond based on what we discussed and I'll get the character created. Level 1 to start? I can't remember for sure.&nbsp;</div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 3:38 PM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">Also - what do you think would be useful language choices for someone who's spent decades traveling and merchanting and now more years looking for lore and readings on the Moon? I figured Elvish would be logical on top of Dwarven, Sembaran and Common because the Elves traditionally have a lot of lore about the moon, but beyond that I'm a bit unclear&nbsp;what would make sense.</div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 3:34 PM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr">Awesome! good tweaks. I was trying to make the moon connection more indirect, that one of the Bharazel had essentially pushed him into this new path on behalf of the moon? or perhaps out of curiosity of it themselves? or something like that? But this works just as well and could still be what he believes to have happened.&nbsp;</div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 1:50 PM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr">I like this in general. It might work better to have the moon a bit more self-directed.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">So…</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">When he went to the heart of the mountain the first time, it wasn’t so much that he felt like there was literally a second figure so much as that he felt a shadowy force almost pulling on him, like it was trying to pull him away from the mountain and up and away from the earth (and away from the mountains).&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">He put it out of his mind as much as he could, but there was always a sense in the back of his mind that he was a little bit on the wrong path.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">One day he was returning from a trading trip south to Maseau and him and his companions headed for an entrance to a dwarven road under the mountains — a mostly disused one, but that was said to be safe. The entrance was high up on the mountainside and his companions set up camp just inside the mountain— but Durgil wanted to see the moon, which was full that night, so camped outside by himself.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">He stayed up late watching the moonlight on the mountain peaks and doesn’t remember drifting off. But when he awoke it seemed to still be night, and he seemed to be high up on a mountain peak — similar to where he fell asleep but subtly different somehow.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">He could see a path leading toward the peak, and a doorway to the mountain road where his companions were camped. He knew that taking the path would lead him back to the Heart of the Mountain, and that taking it would be accepting a gift? Or obeying an order? Or paying a price? He isn’t quite sure, but he knows that heading up the peak rather than back into mountain would reject the choice.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">He takes the path and ends up Back at the Heart of the Mountain but it is bathed in bright moonlight and all he sees is swirling runes everywhere. He sees for a brief moment a vision of the heavens with lines and runes connecting the moon to many planes and at the heart of it a: Creature? Tomb? Statue? Pyramid? Warrior? Sword? Eye? Heart? He feels like he understands everything! And then the vision collapses and he can barely remember it other than as this feeling of perfect knowledge and satisfaction.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">He takes a new name and thuhr as you write below and it largely continues from there as you wrote.&nbsp;</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr">Mike</div><div dir="ltr"><br></div><div dir="ltr"><br><br><div dir="ltr">Sent from my iPhone</div><div dir="ltr"><br><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 30, 2022, at 11:57 AM, Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿Any comments?<br><br><div dir="ltr">Sent from my iPhone</div><div dir="ltr"><br>Begin forwarded message:<br><br></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><b>From:</b> Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt;<br><b>Date:</b> December 30, 2022 at 11:52:16 AM EST<br><b>To:</b> Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt;<br><b>Subject:</b> <b>Re: Lunar Sorceror</b><br><br></div></blockquote><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿<div><div><div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)">Ok let me know how this lands for you.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)"><br></div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)">Durgil was born some 200 years ago and never quite fit into the role his family and clan made for him. When he went through his naming ritual he earned his Thuhr as one of the Travelers, followers of Maganna, but something was always odd about his experience as he swore there was a second figure in the shadows when he was given his Thuhr. His family told him no one has more than one Thuhr and to put it out of his mind. He settled into a role of a traveling merchant for the people of Nidzahar making deals for mineral sales and crafted works and acquiring food and other finer things from the far and wide. His travels as welcome as they were gave him much time to think and he often found himself staring up at the stars and thinking of the world beyond the dwarven halls he was supposed to be missing. Far from being comfortable when he returned from a trip he tended to itch to get back out on the road but never really able to articulate why.</div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)"><br></div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)">One day after a particularly long trip he was in the foothills of the mountain and found himself for a second time transported to the Heart of the Mountain. This time a different figure bade him join a new Thuhr and take on a new name. He was bid to study with the Runecrafters and scholars and take on a new name of Izgil. Once accepted into the new Thuhr he was to study and learn the ways of the moon and bring his knowledge to any who would listen.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)"><br></div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)">Upsetting the normal order of things and alienating his friends for over 100 years, Izgil changed Thuhr and began studying the moon and it's history and mechanisms. He learned to channel its own energies for rudimentary spells and even began working on new magical theory for how the metaphysical tides of the moon could be exploited for magical means. But he quickly found the knowledge available ran out and was rudimentary at best. He needed new angles and new sources of knowledge. And so he set out for the road again and has been traveling for a few years now. He had been refining his theories and gathering more stories and details. He tends to not discriminate between mysticism, science and lore as he believes there is just as much truth potentially is magical approaches as in the science of the moon though he approaches each as a purely academic study.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)"><br></div><div dir="auto" style="border-color:rgb(0,0,0);color:rgb(0,0,0)">Most recently he learned of the crafters in the settlement of Clenseau who are said to be making a device that would allow him to inspect the details of the surface of the moon itself and so he goes to learn more and broaden his knowledge, happy to once again be sleeping under the stars...</div></div></div></div><div><div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 10:47 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">I want to keep the moon mysterious and a bit vague, in the spirit&nbsp;of the lore I sent you. That is, it isn't a personality that talks to you or has wishes or desires&nbsp;that you really know. It would more be a source of power, in that you have learned to tap into the magical influence of the moon to create spells. You'd probably have a better sense of the mechanics of the moon than its personality, which is entirely a mystery to you.<div><br></div><div>There are many many stories about the origins and true nature of the moon and you would have little sense of what is or is not true. The bit you would understand a bit better is the nature of planar tides. The moon influences how the planes interact with Taelgar in an important way. When it is full, it pulls on the spiritual realms (the homes of the gods, and places like Arborea, Pandemonium, and the Nine Hells -- the Outer Planes in traditional D&amp;D sense) and draws them closer to Taelgar itself. When it is new, the tides pull the opposite way, so to speak, and it pulls the elemental planes, the Feywild, and the Shadowfell and related planes closer.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>You've learned to tap into whatever magical power of the moon is driving this pulling and shifting of the planar balance and channel it into spells. So the source of your magic is almost not even the moon per se, but rather than energy of the magical tides.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>If it works for your character conception the vision I had of a lunar sorcerer was somewhat more scientific than mystical, so to speak.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>There are communities or groups that do have a more mystical connection to various legends and myths around the moon, and there would also be astronomers and scholars who study the moon, although neither of these are likely to be big in the dwarf community.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>I was trying to avoid making up too much detail about the moon this week but I can if you feel you would rather not go the science-y route and want something more mystical.</div><div><br></div><div>A couple of ideas for motivations....</div><div><br></div><div>You could be seeking information or things - there is a small dwarven community in Clenseau, and it is known for its smithing.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>** Maybe there is a dwarf there who specializes in rare metals that fell from the sky and you want to meet them.&nbsp;</div><div>** Or a dwarf or human who builds strange devices, and you heard a rumor of some telescope like thing that they were building.&nbsp;</div><div>** Or maybe an eccentric human who has been mapping the phases and orbit of the moon for fifty years that you heard of somehow.&nbsp;</div><div>** Or there could be a rumor of a small god (a <i>kestavo</i>, one of the spirits that humans used to worship that are now mostly gone) that was the patron of a small spring that glowed with a mystical light in the moonlight, and you are looking for it, hoping to see if you could commune with the spirit and learn more about the moon.</div><div><br></div><div>You could have a non-moon connection to the dwarven community. A brother, sister, cousin, uncle, aunt, etc who lives there, and who is going to give you a place to live/be. If for example you were a young dwarf who wanted to leave the mountains, heading somewhere like Clenseau could be a sensible place to be going -- just because you want to settle somewhere where you can see stars. (Although it is worth pointing out that dwarven cities are not entirely underground,&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>You could be seeking money, and heard of someone in Clenseau&nbsp;offering a job or some sort of funding, which you need for your research.</div><div><br></div><div>There is also no reason you have to be young. Maybe you came to the moon-research in your mid-life, and have a personal connection to Clenseau from a hundred years ago. You could be returning after spending some time in the mountains.</div><div><br></div><div>You could be chasing someone or something -- a friend / sibling / etc who is lost and the trail leads you to Clenseau.</div><div><br></div><div>I can come up with more ideas if nothing above inspires.</div><div><br></div></div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:38 AM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">Also for character hooks I'll probably read through the world bits you have on dwarves but I'm cool going with the solo champion bit. I think that would require being a bit unusual for a dwarf, sort of Moana is to the Sea as this dwarf is to the moon and sky. Always sneaking out to see the moon etc and as he ages he's reading and researching more about it and how it works. His studies eventually lead him into magic and he by some circumstance forms this bond with the moon.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">From there I'm a bit fuzzy. Does the moon have an agenda I'm supposed to advance? I can determine an internal reason for traveling (trying to find others who have connected to the moon could be an easy one) but also if once this bond is formed I'd learn anything that would motivate me that would help</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:30 AM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div>Awesome, thanks!</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:21 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">You should use one of the Taelgar dwarf&nbsp;options.<div><br></div><div>You can read more about Dwarves here:&nbsp;<a href="https://www.gmbinder.com/share/-NALzGmeZSQO2O9GMtLD" target="_blank">https://www.gmbinder.com/share/-NALzGmeZSQO2O9GMtLD</a></div><div><br></div><div>Me and my brother have made two versions of dwarves on D&amp;D&nbsp;Beyond for Taelgar. There is one based on the One D&amp;D Playtest stuff here&nbsp;<a href="https://www.dndbeyond.com/races/1147048-dwarf-taelgar-playtest" target="_blank">https://www.dndbeyond.com/races/1147048-dwarf-taelgar-playtest</a></div><div><br></div><div>and an older set of dwarf&nbsp;races based on each caste (Farmer, Warrior, etc). If you enable Homebrew and then look at the homebrew dwarf options you can see them all.</div><div><br></div><div>Pick either the new way or the old way, although the older way might be easier. Either way you will only get a&nbsp;+2 and a&nbsp;+1 or three&nbsp;+1s for ability&nbsp;scores.</div></div><div dir="ltr"><div><br></div><div>Mike</div></div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:04 AM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">Hmm that sounds fun. I didn't think of switching the spellcasting stat. Good idea!</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">If I go mountain dwarf I think with the custom origin rules they can two +2 stat increases. Would you be ok if I use them for int and con in that case?</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 8:50 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">One random thought...<div><br></div><div>The entire party right now is CHA-focused at least a little bit... if you wanted to play an INT-focused sorcerer I'd be happy to make that work. That is, a sorcerer who's spellcasting stat is INT rather than CHA. An INT-based lunar dwarven sorcerer who got magic by studying the lunar cycles would be a good fit to the rest of the party and seems like it could be fun.</div><div><br></div><div>But it's just a random&nbsp;idea, you are welcome to keep up the CHA-trend in the party too. That will work fine :)&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div><br></div></div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Thu, Dec 29, 2022 at 2:07 PM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto"><br><div dir="ltr"><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 29, 2022, at 12:30 PM, Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿<div dir="auto">A couple of thoughts:</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">1. How would you feel about making the moon's blessing cantrip a spell attack instead of a save or suck? It's probably equivalent from a math perspective but it just feels better to be in control of whether it hits or not.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>Fine with me.&nbsp;</div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">2. For the spell lists we could look at the book for inspiration I think the bonus spells in particular we can pull heavily from there. They use a mix of spells including some not normally on the sorcerer's spell list if I recall correctly. May as well not reinvent the wheel.</div><div dir="auto"><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>I can look especially if you want to send me a screenshot of that section or something. But also there is a specific Taelgar phenomenon at work here — during the new moon the planar tides pull strongly at the elemental and fey planes, so elemental and fey magic is stronger. During the full moon the tides are pulling the divine/spiritual realms closer, so more divine flavored magic is stronger. This isn’t a phenomenon that affects all spellcasters but it would impact this particular subclass so I want to try to align the bonus spells to that theory.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">3. For lunar vision normally that would stack on top of dark vision but it's slightly different, so of i choose a race with dark vision does it become 90 or are they different? If it stacks i would just say "gain dark vision up to 30 feet and in addition moonlight counts as daylight for you" so the rest of it docks into the RAW more easily.</div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>Let’s discuss if it comes up. I won’t make it useless if you end up taking a dwarf though.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">4. For the 6th level feature I love the flavor. I wonder if there's a way to make it not totally useless in the opposite phase for a given spell? It would feel unfortunate if the one spell I needed just was out of phase for this. Like would it be balanced to divvy up the metamagic feats themselves instead of the spells colleges? That way I can reduce the cost of these metamagics on half the month and vice versa but the spells can be whatever.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>I don’t love the game flavor of dividing up metamagic as the point is to align the spells with the planar tides described above. But if the flip-flop nature seems annoying I can brainstorm other ideas. It will be a while before the 6th level feature comes into play.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">Race I'm debating a bit. Originally leaning towards human, but also considering Dwarf. Alternatively there are a couple of fun races that offer flight which is fun but I don't know if any of them work in this world. </div></div></blockquote><div><br></div><div>I’m open to anything but human or dwarf is certainly easier to fit into the world.&nbsp;</div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="auto">Background would depend a bit on the race obviously but I'm thinking some kind of druidic / priesthood style order of the moon? Is there room for that sort of thing? Or would I be more of a solo champion?</div><div><br></div></div></blockquote><div><br></div>Again, open to anything but a solo champion so to speak fits into the plot a bit better.&nbsp;<div><br></div><div>Mike<br><div><br></div><br><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Thu, Dec 29, 2022 at 10:54 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="ltr">Matt,<div><br></div><div>I haven't made up more than the initial bits, but here is what I'm thinking for lunar sorcerer. The version below is only made up through 6th level and leans heavily into different abilities for different phases of the moon. I am happy to set up a moon calendar so we know which phases of the moon are which (probably by just picking an Earth year and using that) but I can't quite decide if it will be too annoying to track.</div><div><br></div><div>What do you think of the below? I'm still tweaking a bit so might play with the power levels a bit and totally happy to get feedback. If you like it I'll make it in D&amp;D Beyond.</div><div><br></div><div><b>1st Level Power</b></div><div>You gain a new cantrip, <i>moon's blessing</i>, that allows you to call forth the power of the moon to attack your enemies, and additionally, you gain a set of bonus spells.</div><div><br></div><div><b><i>Moon's Blessing</i></b></div><div>You call forth the blessing of the moon to attack your enemies. You choose an enemy within 60', who must make a DEX save or take 1d8 damage. The spell damage increases by 1d8 at 5th, 11th, and 17th level.</div><div><br></div><div>During the full moon, and the week around it, the lunar tides pull strongly on the planes of the gods, and the damage is radiant, and you gain the ability to target a second creature within 5'.</div><div><br></div><div>During the new moon, and the week around it, the lunar tides pull strongly on the elemental planes, the damage is elemental, channeled from the elemental planes. You may choose which plane to channel when you cast the spell:&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>From the plane of fire, the spell does fire damage.</div><div>From the plane of water, the spell does cold damage.</div><div>From the plane of air, the spell does thunder or lightning damage, your choice</div><div>From the plane of earth, the spell does bludgeoning damage and deals double damage to object or structures</div><div><br></div><div>In between, when the moon is a crescent, the tides are balanced, and the spell does force damage, and has a range of 90' instead of 60'</div><div><br></div><div><b><i>Lunar Vision</i></b></div><div>You gain the ability to see in the moonlight as well as the daylight. You can see in dim light as well as full light, and in darkness, out to a range of 30'</div><div><b><br></b></div><div><b>Bonus Spells</b></div><div><b><br></b></div><div>You gain three bonus spells for each level, which count as sorcerer spells for you, and which do not count towards your known spells. These spells are divided by phase of the moon: one spell of each level is a full moon spell, one a new moon spell, and one a neutral spell. During the week around the new moon, and the week of the waning moon before that, you may not cast the full moon spell. During the week of the full moon, and the week of the waxing moon before that, you may not cast the new moon spell. So in effect, for half the month you have the new moon spell and half the full moon, plus you always have access to the neutral spell.</div><div><br></div><div>1st Level:</div><div>New Moon: Charm Person</div><div>Full Moon: Sanctuary</div><div>Neutral: TBD</div><div><b><br></b></div><div>3rd Level:</div><div>New Moon: TBD</div><div>etc</div><div><br></div><div><b>6th Level</b></div><div>At 6th level, you start to be able to see and manipulate the lunar planar tides more directly to help your magic.</div><div><br></div><div>When the moon is full, and for the week around it, when casting spells of the Abjuration, Necromancy, Evocation, or Divination schools you can reduce the cost of any metamagic by 1 min 0.</div><div><br></div><div>When the moon is new, and for the week around it, when casting spells of the Enchantment, Conjuration, Illusion, or Transmutation schools you can reduce the cost of any metamagic by 1 min 0.</div><div><br></div><div>You can use this feature a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus, and regain all uses after a long rest.</div><div><br></div><div>[Obviously this effect is useless half the month; either I will expand so that each version is 50% of the time, or split the spells into 3 groups with one group for the middle, or add some other ability for the middle point in time.. any thoughts?]</div><div><br></div><div>Haven't thought about 14th or higher but obviously a long way away :)</div><div><br></div><div>Any thoughts on race and background?&nbsp;</div></div><div dir="ltr"><div><br></div><div>Mike</div></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</div></blockquote></div></div></blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</div>
</div>
</div></blockquote></div></blockquote></div></div></div></div></div></div></blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div>
</div></blockquote></div></div></blockquote></div></div>
</blockquote></div>
</blockquote></div></div>
</div></blockquote></div></body></html>

## Sat, 31 Dec 2022 11:09:43 -0500 — Matthew Rand <rand.matt@gmail.com>

Subject: Re: Lunar Sorceror

1. Chaos bolt vs dissonant whispers. I like the flavor of dissonant
whispers a lot and if you have a justification for it to be on the
elemental end because that's where the feywild lives I think that's fine.
If you want a more elemental theme throughout then chaos bolt does make
more sense.

2. Moon's pull sounds fun. The persistent slow adds some nice utility to
it.

 >>>>>>>> I want to keep the moon mysterious and a bit vague, in the
 >>>>>>>> spirit of the lore I sent you. That is, it isn't a personality that talks
 >>>>>>>> to you or has wishes or desires that you really know. It would more be a
 >>>>>>>> source of power, in that you have learned to tap into the magical influence
 >>>>>>>> of the moon to create spells. You'd probably have a better sense of the
 >>>>>>>> mechanics of the moon than its personality, which is entirely a mystery to
 >>>>>>>> you.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> There are many many stories about the origins and true nature of
 >>>>>>>> the moon and you would have little sense of what is or is not true. The bit
 >>>>>>>> you would understand a bit better is the nature of planar tides. The moon
 >>>>>>>> influences how the planes interact with Taelgar in an important way. When
 >>>>>>>> it is full, it pulls on the spiritual realms (the homes of the gods, and
 >>>>>>>> places like Arborea, Pandemonium, and the Nine Hells -- the Outer Planes in
 >>>>>>>> traditional D&D sense) and draws them closer to Taelgar itself. When it is
 >>>>>>>> new, the tides pull the opposite way, so to speak, and it pulls the
 >>>>>>>> elemental planes, the Feywild, and the Shadowfell and related planes
 >>>>>>>> closer.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> You've learned to tap into whatever magical power of the moon is
 >>>>>>>> driving this pulling and shifting of the planar balance and channel it into
 >>>>>>>> spells. So the source of your magic is almost not even the moon per se, but
 >>>>>>>> rather than energy of the magical tides.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> If it works for your character conception the vision I had of a
 >>>>>>>> lunar sorcerer was somewhat more scientific than mystical, so to speak.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> There are communities or groups that do have a more mystical
 >>>>>>>> connection to various legends and myths around the moon, and there would
 >>>>>>>> also be astronomers and scholars who study the moon, although neither of
 >>>>>>>> these are likely to be big in the dwarf community.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> I was trying to avoid making up too much detail about the moon this
 >>>>>>>> week but I can if you feel you would rather not go the science-y route and
 >>>>>>>> want something more mystical.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> A couple of ideas for motivations....
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> You could be seeking information or things - there is a small
 >>>>>>>> dwarven community in Clenseau, and it is known for its smithing.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> ** Maybe there is a dwarf there who specializes in rare metals that
 >>>>>>>> fell from the sky and you want to meet them.
 >>>>>>>> ** Or a dwarf or human who builds strange devices, and you heard a
 >>>>>>>> rumor of some telescope like thing that they were building.
 >>>>>>>> ** Or maybe an eccentric human who has been mapping the phases and
 >>>>>>>> orbit of the moon for fifty years that you heard of somehow.
 >>>>>>>> ** Or there could be a rumor of a small god (a *kestavo*, one of
 >>>>>>>> the spirits that humans used to worship that are now mostly gone) that was
 >>>>>>>> the patron of a small spring that glowed with a mystical light in the
 >>>>>>>> moonlight, and you are looking for it, hoping to see if you could commune
 >>>>>>>> with the spirit and learn more about the moon.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> You could have a non-moon connection to the dwarven community. A
 >>>>>>>> brother, sister, cousin, uncle, aunt, etc who lives there, and who is going
 >>>>>>>> to give you a place to live/be. If for example you were a young dwarf who
 >>>>>>>> wanted to leave the mountains, heading somewhere like Clenseau could be a
 >>>>>>>> sensible place to be going -- just because you want to settle somewhere
 >>>>>>>> where you can see stars. (Although it is worth pointing out that dwarven
 >>>>>>>> cities are not entirely underground,
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> You could be seeking money, and heard of someone in
 >>>>>>>> Clenseau offering a job or some sort of funding, which you need for your
 >>>>>>>> research.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> There is also no reason you have to be young. Maybe you came to the
 >>>>>>>> moon-research in your mid-life, and have a personal connection to Clenseau
 >>>>>>>> from a hundred years ago. You could be returning after spending some time
 >>>>>>>> in the mountains.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> You could be chasing someone or something -- a friend / sibling /
 >>>>>>>> etc who is lost and the trail leads you to Clenseau.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> I can come up with more ideas if nothing above inspires.
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>> On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:38 AM Matthew Rand <rand.matt@gmail.com>
 >>>>>>>> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>> Also for character hooks I'll probably read through the world bits
 >>>>>>>>> you have on dwarves but I'm cool going with the solo champion bit. I think
 >>>>>>>>> that would require being a bit unusual for a dwarf, sort of Moana is to the
 >>>>>>>>> Sea as this dwarf is to the moon and sky. Always sneaking out to see the
 >>>>>>>>> moon etc and as he ages he's reading and researching more about it and how
 >>>>>>>>> it works. His studies eventually lead him into magic and he by some
 >>>>>>>>> circumstance forms this bond with the moon.
 >>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>> From there I'm a bit fuzzy. Does the moon have an agenda I'm
 >>>>>>>>> supposed to advance? I can determine an internal reason for traveling
 >>>>>>>>> (trying to find others who have connected to the moon could be an easy one)
 >>>>>>>>> but also if once this bond is formed I'd learn anything that would motivate
 >>>>>>>>> me that would help
 >>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>> On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:30 AM Matthew Rand <rand.matt@gmail.com>
 >>>>>>>>> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>> Awesome, thanks!
 >>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>> On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:21 AM Mike Sackton <msackton@gmail.com>
 >>>>>>>>>> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> You should use one of the Taelgar dwarf options.
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> You can read more about Dwarves here:
 >>>>>>>>>>> https://www.gmbinder.com/share/-NALzGmeZSQO2O9GMtLD
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> Me and my brother have made two versions of dwarves on
 >>>>>>>>>>> D&D Beyond for Taelgar. There is one based on the One D&D Playtest stuff
 >>>>>>>>>>> here
 >>>>>>>>>>> https://www.dndbeyond.com/races/1147048-dwarf-taelgar-playtest
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> and an older set of dwarf races based on each caste (Farmer,
 >>>>>>>>>>> Warrior, etc). If you enable Homebrew and then look at the homebrew dwarf
 >>>>>>>>>>> options you can see them all.
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> Pick either the new way or the old way, although the older way
 >>>>>>>>>>> might be easier. Either way you will only get a +2 and a +1 or three +1s
 >>>>>>>>>>> for ability scores.
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> Mike
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>> On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 9:04 AM Matthew Rand <
 >>>>>>>>>>> rand.matt@gmail.com> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>> Hmm that sounds fun. I didn't think of switching the
 >>>>>>>>>>>> spellcasting stat. Good idea!
 >>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>> If I go mountain dwarf I think with the custom origin rules
 >>>>>>>>>>>> they can two +2 stat increases. Would you be ok if I use them for int and
 >>>>>>>>>>>> con in that case?
 >>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>> On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 8:50 AM Mike Sackton <
 >>>>>>>>>>>> msackton@gmail.com> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> One random thought...
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> The entire party right now is CHA-focused at least a little
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> bit... if you wanted to play an INT-focused sorcerer I'd be happy to make
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> that work. That is, a sorcerer who's spellcasting stat is INT rather than
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> CHA. An INT-based lunar dwarven sorcerer who got magic by studying the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> lunar cycles would be a good fit to the rest of the party and seems like it
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> could be fun.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> But it's just a random idea, you are welcome to keep up the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> CHA-trend in the party too. That will work fine :)
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> On Thu, Dec 29, 2022 at 2:07 PM Mike Sackton <
 >>>>>>>>>>>>> msackton@gmail.com> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> On Dec 29, 2022, at 12:30 PM, Matthew Rand <
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> rand.matt@gmail.com> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> A couple of thoughts:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> 1. How would you feel about making the moon's blessing
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> cantrip a spell attack instead of a save or suck? It's probably equivalent
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> from a math perspective but it just feels better to be in control of
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> whether it hits or not.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> Fine with me.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> 2. For the spell lists we could look at the book for
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> inspiration I think the bonus spells in particular we can pull heavily from
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> there. They use a mix of spells including some not normally on the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> sorcerer's spell list if I recall correctly. May as well not reinvent the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> wheel.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> I can look especially if you want to send me a screenshot of
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> that section or something. But also there is a specific Taelgar phenomenon
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> at work here — during the new moon the planar tides pull strongly at the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> elemental and fey planes, so elemental and fey magic is stronger. During
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> the full moon the tides are pulling the divine/spiritual realms closer, so
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> more divine flavored magic is stronger. This isn’t a phenomenon that
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> affects all spellcasters but it would impact this particular subclass so I
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> want to try to align the bonus spells to that theory.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> 3. For lunar vision normally that would stack on top of dark
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> vision but it's slightly different, so of i choose a race with dark vision
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> does it become 90 or are they different? If it stacks i would just say
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> "gain dark vision up to 30 feet and in addition moonlight counts as
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> daylight for you" so the rest of it docks into the RAW more easily.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> Let’s discuss if it comes up. I won’t make it useless if you
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> end up taking a dwarf though.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> 4. For the 6th level feature I love the flavor. I wonder if
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> there's a way to make it not totally useless in the opposite phase for a
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> given spell? It would feel unfortunate if the one spell I needed just was
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> out of phase for this. Like would it be balanced to divvy up the metamagic
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> feats themselves instead of the spells colleges? That way I can reduce the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> cost of these metamagics on half the month and vice versa but the spells
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> can be whatever.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> I don’t love the game flavor of dividing up metamagic as the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> point is to align the spells with the planar tides described above. But if
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> the flip-flop nature seems annoying I can brainstorm other ideas. It will
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> be a while before the 6th level feature comes into play.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> Race I'm debating a bit. Originally leaning towards human,
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> but also considering Dwarf. Alternatively there are a couple of fun races
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> that offer flight which is fun but I don't know if any of them work in this
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> world.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> I’m open to anything but human or dwarf is certainly easier
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> to fit into the world.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> Background would depend a bit on the race obviously but I'm
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> thinking some kind of druidic / priesthood style order of the moon? Is
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> there room for that sort of thing? Or would I be more of a solo champion?
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> Again, open to anything but a solo champion so to speak fits
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> into the plot a bit better.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> Mike
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> On Thu, Dec 29, 2022 at 10:54 AM Mike Sackton <
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>> msackton@gmail.com> wrote:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Matt,
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> I haven't made up more than the initial bits, but here is
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> what I'm thinking for lunar sorcerer. The version below is only made up
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> through 6th level and leans heavily into different abilities for different
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> phases of the moon. I am happy to set up a moon calendar so we know which
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> phases of the moon are which (probably by just picking an Earth year and
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> using that) but I can't quite decide if it will be too annoying to track.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> What do you think of the below? I'm still tweaking a bit so
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> might play with the power levels a bit and totally happy to get feedback.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> If you like it I'll make it in D&D Beyond.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> *1st Level Power*
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> You gain a new cantrip, *moon's blessing*, that allows you
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> to call forth the power of the moon to attack your enemies, and
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> additionally, you gain a set of bonus spells.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> *Moon's Blessing*
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> You call forth the blessing of the moon to attack your
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> enemies. You choose an enemy within 60', who must make a DEX save or take
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> 1d8 damage. The spell damage increases by 1d8 at 5th, 11th, and 17th level.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> During the full moon, and the week around it, the lunar
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> tides pull strongly on the planes of the gods, and the damage is radiant,
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> and you gain the ability to target a second creature within 5'.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> During the new moon, and the week around it, the lunar tides
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> pull strongly on the elemental planes, the damage is elemental, channeled
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> from the elemental planes. You may choose which plane to channel when you
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> cast the spell:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> From the plane of fire, the spell does fire damage.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> From the plane of water, the spell does cold damage.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> From the plane of air, the spell does thunder or lightning
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> damage, your choice
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> From the plane of earth, the spell does bludgeoning damage
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> and deals double damage to object or structures
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> In between, when the moon is a crescent, the tides are
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> balanced, and the spell does force damage, and has a range of 90' instead
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> of 60'
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> *Lunar Vision*
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> You gain the ability to see in the moonlight as well as the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> daylight. You can see in dim light as well as full light, and in darkness,
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> out to a range of 30'
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> *Bonus Spells*
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> You gain three bonus spells for each level, which count as
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> sorcerer spells for you, and which do not count towards your known spells.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> These spells are divided by phase of the moon: one spell of each level is a
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> full moon spell, one a new moon spell, and one a neutral spell. During the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> week around the new moon, and the week of the waning moon before that, you
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> may not cast the full moon spell. During the week of the full moon, and the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> week of the waxing moon before that, you may not cast the new moon spell.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> So in effect, for half the month you have the new moon spell and half the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> full moon, plus you always have access to the neutral spell.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> 1st Level:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> New Moon: Charm Person
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Full Moon: Sanctuary
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Neutral: TBD
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> 3rd Level:
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> New Moon: TBD
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> etc
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> *6th Level*
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> At 6th level, you start to be able to see and manipulate the
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> lunar planar tides more directly to help your magic.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> When the moon is full, and for the week around it, when
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> casting spells of the Abjuration, Necromancy, Evocation, or Divination
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> schools you can reduce the cost of any metamagic by 1 min 0.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> When the moon is new, and for the week around it, when
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> casting spells of the Enchantment, Conjuration, Illusion, or Transmutation
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> schools you can reduce the cost of any metamagic by 1 min 0.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> You can use this feature a number of times equal to your
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> proficiency bonus, and regain all uses after a long rest.
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> [Obviously this effect is useless half the month; either I
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> will expand so that each version is 50% of the time, or split the spells
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> into 3 groups with one group for the middle, or add some other ability for
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> the middle point in time.. any thoughts?]
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Haven't thought about 14th or higher but obviously a long
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> way away :)
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Any thoughts on race and background?
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>> Mike
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>>
 >>>>>>>>>>>>>>

The “new moon” phase is very much Shadowfell/Feywild + elemental planes so I like having something that highlights the fey side and dissonant whispers seems very fey to me.&nbsp;<div><div><br><div dir="ltr">Sent from my iPhone</div><div dir="ltr"><br><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 31, 2022, at 11:09 AM, Matthew Rand &lt;rand.matt@gmail.com&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿<div dir="auto">1. Chaos bolt vs dissonant whispers. I like the flavor of dissonant whispers a lot and if you have a justification for it to be on the elemental end because that's where the feywild lives I think that's fine. If you want a more elemental theme throughout then chaos bolt does make more sense.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">2. Moon's pull sounds fun. The persistent slow adds some nice utility to it.&nbsp;</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Sat, Dec 31, 2022 at 9:10 AM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">I was also thinking of tweaking moon’s pull so that instead of a sphere/damage it was something like:<div><br></div><div>Target a single creature or object. It doesn't have everything, in particular it is still missing bonus spells or 6th/14/th/18th level features.<div><br></div><div>I changed the darkvision thing to Lunar Sight -- you can see in moonlight as daylight, plus you get advantage on perception checks at night.</div><div><br></div><div>For bonus spells, for 1st level I was thinking:</div><div><br></div><div>Full Moon: Sanctuary</div><div>New Moon: Dissonant Whispers</div><div>Half-Moon (always): Magnify Gravity</div><div><br></div><div>Thoughts?</div></div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 6:13 PM Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">I'm thinking he wasn't directly involved in the war. To me he basically lived most of his life as an unleveled civilian and only recently did he become anything approaching heroic which is how he's as old as he is and still only level 1.&nbsp;</div><div dir="auto"><br></div><div dir="auto">Also good notes on the languages. I also was looking at the homebrew race template and wasn't sure if it would be cool for him to have two runes he can inscribe (buy has to choose one each day) given his split history. I was thinking the Rune of Lore (more recently) and the Rune of Exploration from his previous life?</div><div><br><div class="gmail_quote"><div dir="ltr" class="gmail_attr">On Fri, Dec 30, 2022 at 4:25 PM Mike Sackton &lt;<a href="mailto:msackton@gmail.com" target="_blank">msackton@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br></div><blockquote class="gmail_quote" style="margin:0px 0px 0px 0.8ex;border-left-width:1px;border-left-style:solid;padding-left:1ex;border-left-color:rgb(204,204,204)"><div dir="auto">Level 1 to start but you’ll level up to 2nd almost immediately.&nbsp;<div><br></div><div>For languages I think Drankorian (the ancient Latin analogue) might be interesting as could giant (there are legends that the giants had interesting runes/scholarship) or Chardonian (the language of the current main scholar city)</div><div><br></div><div>Highlander would also definitely be something he would know— it’s the main language of the humans who live closest to the dwarves.&nbsp;</div><div><br></div><div>Mike</div><div><br><div dir="ltr">Sent from my iPhone</div><div dir="ltr"><br><blockquote type="cite">On Dec 30, 2022, at 3:49 PM, Matthew Rand &lt;<a href="mailto:rand.matt@gmail.com" target="_blank">rand.matt@gmail.com</a>&gt; wrote:<br><br></blockquote></div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr">﻿</div></blockquote></div></div><div dir="auto"><div><blockquote type="cite"><div dir="ltr"><div dir="ltr">And lastly, let me know when you've created the subclass in D&amp;D beyond based on what we discussed and I'll get the character created.
